  interface BeginFields
  readonly BeginFields.kind: "begin"
  readonly BeginFields.frame: number
  readonly BeginFields.sampleRate: SampleRate
  readonly BeginFields.channels: number
  type CaptureEnd = { readonly frame: number; readonly reason: typeof CaptureEndReason.Failed; readonly summary: string; } | { readonly frame: number; readonly reason: typeof CaptureEndReason.Released | typeof CaptureEndReason.Stopped; }
  const CaptureEndReason: { readonly Stopped: "stopped"; readonly Released: "released"; readonly Failed: "failed"; }
  type CaptureEndReason = "failed" | "released" | "stopped"
  const CaptureTransport: { readonly Posted: "posted"; readonly SharedRing: "shared-ring"; }
  type CaptureTransport = "posted" | "shared-ring"
  type CaptureWire = (BeginFields & { readonly transport: typeof CaptureTransport.Posted; }) | (BeginFields & { readonly transport: typeof CaptureTransport.SharedRing; readonly ring: SharedArrayBuffer; }) | ({ readonly kind: typeof CaptureWireKind.End; } & CaptureEnd) | { readonly kind: typeof CaptureWireKind.Block; readonly frame: number; readonly channels: readonly Float32Array[]; } | { readonly kind: typeof CaptureWireKind.Gap; readonly frame: number; readonly frames: number; } | { readonly kind: typeof CaptureWireKind.Written; readonly frame: number; }

  interface ConversationHost
  readonly ConversationHost.post: (message: FromInferenceWorker, transfer: readonly ArrayBuffer[]) => void
  readonly ConversationHost.runtime: () => WorkerRuntime
  class InferenceConversation
  new InferenceConversation(host: ConversationHost): InferenceConversation
  readonly InferenceConversation.#host: ConversationHost
  readonly InferenceConversation.#sessions: Map<number, InferenceSession>
  readonly InferenceConversation.#calls: Map<number, CancellationSource>
  readonly InferenceConversation.#wanted: Map<number, (result: DomainResult<ModelBytes>) => void>
  InferenceConversation.#closed: boolean
  InferenceConversation.receive: (value: unknown) => void
  InferenceConversation.messageFailed: () => void
  InferenceConversation.close: () => void
  InferenceConversation.#open: ({ call, sha256, options, }: Message<typeof ToInferenceWorkerKind.Open>) => Promise<void>
  InferenceConversation.#modelOf: (call: number, signal: CancellationSignal | undefined) => Promise<DomainResult<ModelBytes>>

  InferenceConversation.#faulted: (call: number) => (error: unknown) => void
  InferenceConversation.#begin: (call: number) => CancellationSource
  InferenceConversation.#answer: (call: number, result: DomainFailureResult) => void
  InferenceConversation.#failed: (call: number, failures_0: DomainFailure, ...failures: DomainFailure[]) => void
  InferenceConversation.#post: (message: FromInferenceWorker, transfer: readonly ArrayBuffer[]) => void
  type Message<TKind extends "cancel" | "model" | "open" | "release" | "run"> = Extract<{ readonly kind: typeof ToInferenceWorkerKind.Cancel; readonly call: number; }, { readonly kind: TKind; }> | Extract<{ readonly kind: typeof ToInferenceWorkerKind.Model; readonly call: number; readonly model: ModelBytes; }, { readonly kind: TKind; }> | Extract<{ readonly kind: typeof ToInferenceWorkerKind.Open; readonly call: number; readonly sha256: string; readonly options: import("packages/ml-runtime/src/inference-options").InferenceOptions; }, { readonly kind: TKind; }> | Extract<{ readonly kind: typeof ToInferenceWorkerKind.Release; readonly session: number; }, { readonly kind: TKind; }> | Extract<{ readonly kind: typeof ToInferenceWorkerKind.Run; readonly call: number; readonly session: number; readonly inputs: readonly NamedTensor[]; }, { readonly kind: TKind; }>
  type WorkerRuntime = { readonly kind: "serving"; readonly port: InferencePort; } | { readonly kind: "unavailable"; readonly reason: DomainFailure; }

  type ToInferenceWorker = { readonly kind: typeof ToInferenceWorkerKind.Cancel; readonly call: number; } | { readonly kind: typeof ToInferenceWorkerKind.Model; readonly call: number; readonly model: ModelBytes; } | { readonly kind: typeof ToInferenceWorkerKind.Open; readonly call: number; readonly sha256: string; readonly options: InferenceOptions; } | { readonly kind: typeof ToInferenceWorkerKind.Release; readonly session: number; } | { readonly kind: typeof ToInferenceWorkerKind.Run; readonly call: number; readonly session: number; readonly inputs: readonly NamedTensor[]; }

packages/storage-runtime/src/protocol/operations.ts
  type Answered<TValue> = TValue | Transferring<TValue>
  type Handlers<TTable extends Readonly<Record<string, Operation<unknown, unknown>>>> = { readonly [TName in keyof TTable]: (argument: TTable[TName]["argument"], context: ServeContext) => Promise<Answered<TTable[TName]["answer"]>>; }
  interface Operation<TArgument, TAnswer>
  readonly Operation.argument: TArgument
  readonly Operation.answer: TAnswer
  interface PortSide
  readonly PortSide.operations: Readonly<Record<string, Operation<unknown, unknown>>>
  readonly PortSide.streams: Readonly<Record<string, Stream<unknown>>>
  interface ServeContext
  readonly ServeContext.signal: AbortSignal
  interface Stream<TValue>
  readonly Stream.value: TValue
  class Transferring<TValue>
  new Transferring<TValue>(value: TValue, transfer: readonly Transferable[]): Transferring<TValue>
  readonly Transferring.value: TValue
  readonly Transferring.transfer: readonly Transferable[]

  type PackOperations = { 'packs.installations': Operation<undefined, DomainResult<readonly Installation[]>>; 'packs.catalogue': Operation<CatalogueAt, DomainResult<readonly ModelPackManifest[]>>; 'packs.download': Operation<CatalogueAt & { readonly manifest: ModelPackManifest; }, DomainResult<InstallState>>; 'packs.resume': Operation<CatalogueAt & { readonly ref: PackRef; }, DomainResult<InstallState>>; 'packs.retry': Operation<CatalogueAt & { readonly ref: PackRef; }, DomainResult<InstallState>>; 'packs.pause': Operation<PackRef, DomainResult<void>>; 'packs.import': Operation<{ readonly folder: CrossingFolder; }, DomainResult<PackImport>>; 'packs.cancel': Operation<PackRef, DomainResult<InstallState>>; 'packs.remove': Operation<{ readonly ref: PackRef; readonly knowingly: boolean; }, DomainResult<InstallState>>; 'packs.read': Operation<{ readonly ref: PackRef; readonly path: string; }, DomainResult<KeptFile>>; }

packages/storage-runtime/src/protocol/page-operations.ts
  type BackupCopy = { readonly project: import("packages/domain/src/index").ProjectId; readonly name: string; readonly number: number; readonly at: number; }
  type CrossingBytes = HeldFile | { readonly kind: "port"; readonly port: PagePort; readonly size: number; }
  type CrossingFile = Omit<ExternalFile, "source"> & { readonly bytes: CrossingBytes; }
  type CrossingFolder = { readonly kind: "files"; readonly files: readonly FolderFile[]; } | { readonly kind: "port"; readonly port: PagePort; }
  type CrossingLocated = { readonly kind: "absent"; readonly reason: AbsenceReason; } | { readonly kind: "found"; readonly file: CrossingFile; }
  type Lent<TArgument, TAnswer> = Operation<{ readonly port: PagePort; } & TArgument, TAnswer>
  type PageOperations = { 'sink.write': Lent<{ readonly chunk: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>; }, undefined>; 'sink.close': Lent<unknown, undefined>; 'sink.abort': Lent<{ readonly reason: string | undefined; }, undefined>; 'source.read': Lent<{ readonly offset: number; readonly length: number; }, Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>>; 'folder.list': Lent<unknown, readonly DirectoryFile[]>; 'folder.open': Lent<{ readonly path: string; }, CrossingBytes | undefined>; 'folder.create': Lent<{ readonly path: string; }, PagePort>; 'folder.remove': Lent<{ readonly path: string; }, undefined>; 'backupFolder.create': Lent<{ readonly generation: BackupCopy; }, PagePort>; 'linkedFiles.locate': Lent<{ readonly asset: AssetId; readonly identity: ExternalSourceIdentity; }, CrossingLocated>; }
  type PagePort = number

packages/storage-runtime/src/protocol/port-channel.ts
  interface CallOptions
  readonly CallOptions.signal?: AbortSignal | undefined
  readonly CallOptions.transfer?: readonly Transferable[]
  interface PendingCall
  readonly PendingCall.settle: (outcome: CallOutcome) => void
  readonly PendingCall.reject: (reason: Error) => void
  class PortChannel<TOther extends PortSide, TOwn extends PortSide>
  new PortChannel<TOther extends PortSide, TOwn extends PortSide>(endpoint: PortEndpoint): PortChannel<TOther, TOwn>
  readonly PortChannel.#endpoint: PortEndpoint
  readonly PortChannel.#calls: Map<number, PendingCall>
  readonly PortChannel.#serving: Map<number, AbortController>
  readonly PortChannel.#listeners: Map<string, Set<(value: unknown) => void>>
  PortChannel.#handlers: Handlers<TOwn["operations"]> | undefined
  PortChannel.#nextId: number
  PortChannel.#broken: Error | undefined
  PortChannel.call: <TName extends keyof TOther["operations"] & string>(operation: TName, argument: TOther["operations"][TName]["argument"], options?: CallOptions) => Promise<TOther["operations"][TName]["answer"]>
  PortChannel.serve: (handlers: Handlers<TOwn["operations"]>) => void
  PortChannel.emit: <TName extends keyof TOwn["streams"] & string>(stream: TName, value: TOwn["streams"][TName]["value"], transfer?: readonly Transferable[]) => void
  PortChannel.listen: <TName extends keyof TOther["streams"] & string>(stream: TName, listener: (value: TOther["streams"][TName]["value"]) => void) => () => void
  PortChannel.#post: (message: PortMessage, transfer: readonly Transferable[]) => void
  PortChannel.#receive: (data: unknown) => void
  PortChannel.#settle: (id: number, outcome: CallOutcome) => void
  PortChannel.#serveCall: (id: number, operation: string, argument: unknown) => void
  PortChannel.#outcomeOf: (operation: string, argument: unknown, signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<Transferring<CallOutcome>>
  PortChannel.#answer: (id: number, answer: Transferring<CallOutcome>) => void
  PortChannel.#break: (reason: Error) => void

packages/storage-runtime/src/protocol/port-messages.ts
  type CallOutcome = { readonly kind: "cancelled"; } | { readonly kind: "fault"; readonly message: string; } | { readonly kind: "tree-refused"; readonly failure: TreeFailureKind; readonly message: string; } | { readonly kind: "value"; readonly value: unknown; }
  type PortMessage = { readonly type: "answer"; readonly id: number; readonly outcome: CallOutcome; } | { readonly type: "call"; readonly id: number; readonly operation: string; readonly argument: unknown; } | { readonly type: "cancel"; readonly target: number; } | { readonly type: "event"; readonly stream: string; readonly value: unknown; }

  type ComparisonUpdate = { readonly kind: "closed"; } | { readonly kind: "open"; readonly comparison: Comparison; }
  interface FirstUpdate extends ProjectUpdate

  readonly FirstUpdate.exports: readonly ExportRecord[]

  interface HeldOpening

  type Of<TArgument, TAnswer> = Operation<{ readonly handle: ProjectHandle; } & TArgument, TAnswer>
  type OpeningStream = `opening:${string}`
  type ProjectHandle = number
  interface ProjectOpening extends HeldOpening

  readonly ProjectOpening.report: ProjectRecoveryReport
  readonly ProjectOpening.first: FirstUpdate
  readonly ProjectOpening.slices: number
  type ProjectOperations = { 'projects.open': Of<{ readonly project: ProjectId; readonly access: "read" | "write"; readonly steal?: boolean; }, DomainResult<ProjectOpening>>; 'projects.abandon': Of<unknown, undefined>; 'projects.close': Of<unknown, DomainResult<void>>; 'projects.closeView': Of<unknown, undefined>; 'projects.run': Of<{ readonly invocation: CommandInvocation; }, DomainResult<ChangeOutcome>>; 'projects.runGroup': Of<{ readonly description: string; 'projects.comparedState': Of<{ readonly side: SideName; }, DomainResult<ProjectState>>; 'projects.switchSide': Written<{ readonly side: SideName | undefined; }>; 'projects.closeComparison': Written; 'projects.promote': Written<{ readonly side: SideName; }>; 'projects.planCompaction': Of<{ readonly request: CompactionRequest; }, DomainResult<CompactionPlan>>; 'projects.compactHistory': Written<{ readonly plan: CompactionPlan; readonly confirmation: CompactionConfirmation;   type ProjectStream = `project:${string}`
  interface ProjectUpdate

packages/storage/src/checked-records.ts
  type CheckedReading<TValue> = { readonly kind: "absent"; } | { readonly kind: "invalid"; readonly fault: RecordFault; } | { readonly kind: "valid"; readonly value: TValue; }
  class CheckedRecords
  new CheckedRecords(tree: StorageTree, digest: Digest): CheckedRecords
  readonly CheckedRecords.tree: StorageTree
  readonly CheckedRecords.digest: Digest
  CheckedRecords.write: (path: string, kind: RecordKind, body: JsonValue, signal?: AbortSignal) => Promise<DomainResult<void>>
  CheckedRecords.read: <TValue>(path: string, kind: RecordKind, convert: Converter<TValue>, signal?: AbortSignal) => Promise<CheckedReading<TValue>>
  const RecordKind: { readonly StorageRoot: "storage-root"; readonly ProjectHeader: "project-header"; readonly ProjectHead: "project-head"; readonly Checkpoint: "checkpoint"; readonly HistorySegment: "history-segment"; readonly JournalRecord: "journal-record"; readonly Lease: "lease"; readonly CacheSeal: "cache-seal"; readonly BackupGeneration: "backup-generation"; readonly PackManifest: "pack-manifest"; readonly PackSeal: "pack-seal"; readonly LibraryEntry: "library-entry"; readonly RecordingManifest: "recording-manifest"; }
  type RecordKind = "backup-generation" | "cache-seal" | "checkpoint" | "history-segment" | "journal-record" | "lease" | "library-entry" | "pack-manifest" | "pack-seal" | "project-head" | "project-header" | "recording-manifest" | "storage-root"

  type FromPeakWorker = { readonly kind: typeof FromPeakWorkerKind.Adopted; readonly job: string; readonly bytes: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>; } | { readonly kind: typeof FromPeakWorkerKind.Buckets; readonly job: string; readonly request: number; readonly start: number; readonly frames: number; readonly bucketFrames: number; readonly channels: readonly PeakChannel[]; } | { readonly kind: typeof FromPeakWorkerKind.Complete; readonly job: string; readonly bytes: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>; readonly refusedCache: string | undefined; } | { readonly kind: typeof FromPeakWorkerKind.Failed; readonly job: string; readonly reason: string; } | { readonly kind: typeof FromPeakWorkerKind.Runs; readonly job: string; readonly runs: readonly PeakRun[]; } | { readonly kind: typeof FromPeakWorkerKind.Samples; readonly job: string; readonly request: number; readonly start: number; readonly channels: readonly Float32Array[]; } | { readonly kind: typeof FromPeakWorkerKind.ZeroCrossing; readonly job: string; readonly request: number; readonly position: number | undefined; }

packages/waveform/src/peak-requests.ts
  type PeakRequest = { readonly kind: typeof ToPeakWorkerKind.Buckets; readonly job: string; readonly request: number; readonly range: FrameRange; } | { readonly kind: typeof ToPeakWorkerKind.Samples; readonly job: string; readonly request: number; readonly range: FrameRange; } | { readonly kind: typeof ToPeakWorkerKind.ZeroCrossing; readonly job: string; readonly request: number; readonly position: number; readonly within: number; readonly channels: readonly number[]; }
  class PeakRequests
  new PeakRequests(host: RequestHost): PeakRequests
  readonly PeakRequests.#host: RequestHost
  PeakRequests.#waiting: PeakRequest[]
  PeakRequests.#serving: undefined | { readonly request: PeakRequest; readonly cancel: CancellationSource; }
  PeakRequests.add: (request: PeakRequest) => void
  PeakRequests.cancel: (job: string, request: number) => void
  PeakRequests.forget: (job: string) => void
  PeakRequests.#serve: () => Promise<void>
  PeakRequests.#answer: (request: PeakRequest, cancel: CancellationSource) => Promise<void>
  PeakRequests.#read: (source: PcmSource, range: FrameRange, cancel: CancellationSource, place: (offset: number, frames: number) => AudioFrameBlock, each: (chunk: AudioFrameBlock, offset: number) => void) => Promise<void>
  PeakRequests.#samples: (source: PcmSource, range: FrameRange, cancel: CancellationSource) => Promise<readonly Float32Array[]>
  PeakRequests.#buckets: (source: PcmSource, range: FrameRange, cancel: CancellationSource) => Promise<readonly PeakChannel[]>
  interface RequestHost
  readonly RequestHost.source: (job: string) => PcmSource | undefined
  readonly RequestHost.post: (message: FromPeakWorker) => void
  readonly RequestHost.yieldToHost: () => Promise<void>
  readonly RequestHost.fail: (job: string, reason: string) => void
